([robwestz/ob1_workspace](https://github.com/robwestz/ob1_workspace/tree/HEAD/projects/Bacowr-v6.3))
- **bacteria-fungi-spectrum-classification**: Use when a consensus .mgf of spectra has been batch-searched against microbeMASST and you need to (a) subtract spectra with non-microbial (e.g., human cell line) matches and (b) split the remaining putative microbial spectra by taxonomic origin into bacteria, fungi, or both. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/bacteria-fungi-spectrum-classification))
- **bacterial-community-read-simulation**: Use when you need to reproduce or extend a simulated-community assembly benchmark: you have reference genomes and want to generate in silico metagenomic reads (CAMISIM) and rank assemblers by genome fraction and contiguity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/bacterial-community-read-simulation))
- **bacterial-de-novo-genome-assembly**: Use when benchmarking de novo bacterial genome assembly from basecalled nanopore FASTQ reads, e.g., comparing simulator-derived vs. real reads by QUAST mismatches per 100 kbp and genome fraction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/bacterial-de-novo-genome-assembly))
- **bacterial-genome-allelic-profiling**: Use when you have draft/complete genome FASTA files (e.g. S. agalactiae assemblies) and a cgMLST/wgMLST schema seed, and need to determine allelic profiles or measure how AlleleCall runtime scales with CPU count. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/bacterial-genome-allelic-profiling))
- **bacterial-genome-analysis**: End-to-end orchestration of bacterial genome reconstruction, from raw reads to a fully annotated, high-fidelity genomic sequence. This meta-skill integrates preflight (input validation), assembly, polishing, validation, and annotation into a strict evidence chain based on the nf-core/bacass paradigm. Use when the user wants to assemble, polish, validate, or annotate a bacterial genome — or when they ask "is my bacterial genome ready?". Builds on the upstream read-qc-trimming skill. Pairs with the bettamt-style ask-user stop point pattern from Betta-WGS-agent. ([cheahhl814/bacterial-genome-analysis](https://github.com/cheahhl814/bacterial-genome-analysis/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★1)
- **bacterial-genome-analysis-runner**: Thin Nextflow DSL2 runner (v5.1, opt-in) wrapping the bacterial-genome-analysis bash sub-skills. Mirrors nf-core/bacass resource layout. Use for long-running production runs, HPC/cloud execution, or cohort assembly — not for single-machine dev iteration. Provides 8 modules (SPAdes / Flye / Unicycler / Medaka / Polypolish / Pypolca / QUAST / BUSCO / CheckM / Bakta) + 3 subworkflows (preflight-to-polish, polish-to-qc, qc-to-annotation). Bash recipes remain the source of truth and the default. Triggers: "nextflow bacterial genome", "nextflow bacass", "nextflow assembly pipeline", "nextflow runne… ([cheahhl814/bacterial-genome-analysis](https://github.com/cheahhl814/bacterial-genome-analysis/tree/HEAD/runners/nextflow-runner) · ★1)
- **bacterial-genome-assembly-annotation**: Use when assembling, polishing, annotating, and checking a bacterial isolate genome from long or hybrid reads. Not for AMR calls (amr-genotype-phenotype) or many-genome comparison (pangenome-analysis-prokaryote). ([oumarkme/research_agents](https://github.com/oumarkme/research_agents/tree/HEAD/plugins/bioinfo-lab/skills/bacterial-genome-assembly-annotation))
- **bacterial-genome-assembly-finish-qa**: Use when finishing a bacterial draft assembly from Illumina MiSeq paired-end reads plus Oxford Nanopore long reads, and you must scaffold contigs, fill gaps with repetitive contigs, and verify the npScarf output artifacts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/bacterial-genome-assembly-finish-qa))
- **bacterial-genome-assembly-spades**: Use when processing RIMS-seq (or similar shotgun) paired-end FASTQ reads from a bacterium for which no exact reference genome is available, and an assembled genome is needed for read mapping and motif discovery. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/bacterial-genome-assembly-spades))
- **bacterial-genome-chimera-detection-by-alignment**: Use when validating large assembled contigs (e.g. 342.5 kb and 472.5 kb MeGAMerge contigs) against a reference genome to confirm colinearity or detect chimeric breakpoints. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/bacterial-genome-chimera-detection-by-alignment))
- **bacterial-genome-de-novo-assembly**: Use when paired-end Illumina-style bacterial reads (e.g. RIMS-seq FASTQ pairs from PRJNA706563) must be turned into a reference-free genome assembly, or when alkaline-treated reads must be shown to assemble as well as a DNA-seq(+3H) control. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/bacterial-genome-de-novo-assembly))
- **bacterial-genome-read-alignment**: Use when the input is adapter/quality-trimmed ChIP-seq FASTQ from a bacterial sample and the goal is genome-mapped reads (BAM) as the substrate for transcription factor peak calling with macs2. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/bacterial-genome-read-alignment))
- **bacterial-heat-shock-transformation**: DH5α、GV3101及Ar.Qual化学感受态转化：热激或冻融操作、筛选与周期估算，不适用于电转。 ([biojiayuxin/potato-agent](https://github.com/biojiayuxin/potato-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/potato-wet-lab-protocols/bacterial-heat-shock-transformation) · ★1)
- **bacterial-isolate-genomics-read-processing**: Use when raw paired-end Illumina FASTQ reads from bacterial isolate genomes (e.g., CDC & FDA Antibiotic Resistance Isolate Bank isolates) need quality trimming, adapter removal, contaminant read removal, and merging of overlapping mates before targeted assembly or annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/bacterial-isolate-genomics-read-processing))
- **bacterial-lifestyle-gene-association-analysis**: Use when you have a binary gene cluster absence/presence matrix plus per-genome lifestyle labels and need to enumerate lifestyle-associated genes per group using bacLIFE's 70% prevalence / 2 log2FC rule. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/bacterial-lifestyle-gene-association-analysis))
- **bacterial-lifestyle-prediction**: Use when you have a gene cluster absence/presence matrix for genomes with literature-derived lifestyle labels and need to (a) quantify lifestyle predictability via cross-validated AUC or (b) assign lifestyles to unknown-lifestyle genomes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/bacterial-lifestyle-prediction))
- **bacterial-lipid-class-interpretation**: Use when an annotated bacterial lipidomics feature table (multiple strains/conditions, replicate structure) needs class-level biological interpretation — e.g. verifying that resistance (Dap2 vs Par MRSA) reproduces reported DGDG/LysylPG/fatty-acid-chain-length shifts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/bacterial-lipid-class-interpretation))
- **bacterial-m5c-methylation-sequencing-analysis**: Use when paired-end Illumina fastq.gz reads from heat-alkaline-treated bacterial DNA need m5C motif identification plus shotgun sequencing, or when C-to-T variant enrichment must be quantified per sequence context (e.g. CCWGG) per read mate. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/bacterial-m5c-methylation-sequencing-analysis))
- **bacterial-methylome-pipeline-execution**: Use when raw WGA/native nanopore signal datasets for bacterial strains must be turned into de novo methylation motif calls and compared against REBASE reference motifs or a baseline method such as nanodisco automatic mode. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/bacterial-methylome-pipeline-execution))
- **bacterial-phenotype-prediction**: Use when you have tens or hundreds of bacterial genome sequences (nucleotide or protein FASTAs) and need trait calls for any of Traitar's 67 phenotypes, or when benchmarking genotype-to-phenotype classifiers against curated phenotype labels such as GIDEON or Bergey's Manual entries. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/bacterial-phenotype-prediction))
- **bacterial-producer-strain-annotation**: Use when screening bacterial culture MS data (e.g. HMP isolates, MSV000084475 and MSV000087889) to determine which genera or strains produced specific amino-acid-conjugated bile acids, requiring joint agreement of m/z, retention time, drift time, and isotope dot-product. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/bacterial-producer-strain-annotation))
- **bacterial-reservoir-computing**: Bacterial metabolic models as physical reservoirs for computation. dFBA simulations of microbial growth curves as reservoir states. Separability and similarity metrics predict performance. Activation: reservoir computing, bacterial model, biological computation, dFBA. ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/general-ml/bacterial-reservoir-computing) · ★4)
- **bacterial-trait-prediction-from-genome-sequence**: Use when starting from bacterial nucleotide (or protein) FASTA files for tens to hundreds of genomes and a genotype-to-phenotype assignment across the 67 Traitar trait labels is needed without wet-lab phenotyping. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/bacterial-trait-prediction-from-genome-sequence))
- **bacterial-transcriptional-regulon-quantification**: Use when RNA-seq count matrices from a regulator-deletion versus control strain are available and the goal is to enumerate the regulon (repressed/derepressed gene counts) and confirm specific loci such as luxCDABE are recovered. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/bacterial-transcriptional-regulon-quantification))
- **bacteriologist**: Think and work like an expert Bacteriologist. Use when a task calls for Bacteriologist judgment. Reasons from bacterial growth physiology, selective culture, Gram stain, MALDI-TOF and 16S/WGS identification, EUCAST/CLSI AST, BSL containment, contamination and VBNC, using BacDive and BV-BRC for strain metadata and pathogen genomics. - **bailian-memory**: 个人长期记忆与首次启用引导。当个人背景、偏好或历史会影响答案时使用，包括饮食/出行推荐、 个人计划、按用户习惯写邮件、延续之前的任务；不要求用户说“记得”或点名百炼。 用户提供长期事实、纠正偏好（“以后自然一点”）、确认长期决定（“就选第二种，以后都这样”）， 或要求记住、回忆、查看、忘记、暂停、恢复时也应读取本 Skill；结合前文识别，不能只看最后一句。 先检查本地状态；未配置引导启用，授权后检索和抽取生活/工作记忆。宿主内置记忆不排除此 Skill。 也处理明确的 bl memory 资源操作。纯计算、通用知识、无个性化需要的翻译/写作不触发；授权前不上传。 ([modelstudioai/cli](https://github.com/modelstudioai/cli/tree/HEAD/skills/bailian-memory) · ★372 · 3 copies)

- **bailian-opd**: Guided end-to-end pure Model OPD distillation (pg_opd) on Alibaba Cloud Bailian: authorize → dataset validate → parameter probes → dry-run consent gate → confirmed submit → verified terminal state, driven by deterministic scripts.
